# BACKLOG-1 (lane audit2-backlog-merge): the merged actionable backlog of the complete audit, second pass, 2026-10-11

## How the rows were derived (rules applied, so the counts can be re-derived)

ACTIONABLE = (a) a register-table or fenced-row STATUS of HALF-SLICE, DEAD, UNPROVEN, UNWIRED, STALE or SUPERSEDED, or LIVE carrying a defect qualifier ("LIVE (partly STALE)", "LIVE (with defect)"); (b) a "FROM THE BODY" / body / own-finding / "new findings" / "findings added" / "body facts" / "cross-cutting" cell or bullet in which at least one clause carries a defect word and no "handled / surfaced / intentional / none" negation (clause-level, so a clean sentence in the same cell is not carried); (c) a PRIOR cell or DISAGREES list entry reading DISAGREES, and a prior-audit reconciliation row whose verdict is DISAGREES or REFUTED (prior claim to correct in its file); (d) every STILL-OPEN prior finding (rows or bullets under a "Prior findings still open" heading, and reconciliation rows with STILL-OPEN) except entries that also say FIXED-SINCE without STILL-OPEN; (e) every [HYPOTHESIS] token whose text gives a reason, and every entry of an "Unverified" list not marked RESOLVED; (f) the "ten rows that matter most" bullets that carry a defect clause; (g) MEM-1 duplicate clusters, contradictions, rules citing absent things, and rules whose STILL TRUE is not YES; (h) D2 docs not indexed; (i) test files that fail when run alone.
KIND mapping: STATUS HALF-SLICE -> HALF-SLICE; DEAD and UNWIRED -> DEAD; UNPROVEN -> UNPROVEN; STALE and SUPERSEDED -> STALE-DOC (stale object, doc, migration or skill section); DISAGREES / REFUTED / register-correction -> PRIOR-AUDIT-CORRECTION; [HYPOTHESIS] with a reason -> HYPOTHESIS-TO-EXECUTE; duplicate clusters and "duplicated logic" columns -> DUPLICATE-RULE (rule or logic); SECURITY / DATA-INTEGRITY only when the finding text states a dropped or discarded error, fire-and-forget write, double-apply, missing dedupe key, authorization-order, token-in-log, privilege-by-entry-point or fail-open guard defect (pattern list in build.js, negations for FIXED-SINCE, "handled", "no exposure", test descriptions); all other body, new and STILL-OPEN findings -> by keyword (no caller -> DEAD, no reader/writer -> HALF-SLICE, no test -> UNPROVEN, header/comment/doc drift outside product code -> STALE-DOC), default PRODUCT-DEFECT. KIND is therefore a keyword classification of the register text, not a re-verification; the register row it came from is cited in SOURCE.
- De-duplication: same OBJECT + same KIND + (same status item, or word-overlap >= 0.45 of the finding) is one row, all sources listed. Identical [HYPOTHESIS] text on 6+ rows (boilerplate such as "smoke registration not checked per file") is one row with the count. Status items of a base register superseded by a whole-read re-check register (A4 -> A4a-d, B1 -> B1a-c, C3 -> C3a-c) were dropped when the re-check re-rated the object (49 items; the pairs are in COVERAGE-2026-10-11.md under contradictions).
- PRIOR-AUDIT-CORRECTION also holds the corrections a whole-read re-check lane (A4a-d, B1a-c, C3a-c) made to the base register of this same pass ("[register correction vs A4]" and "[DISAGREES]" lines of those lanes): the base register file must be corrected in the same way as a prior audit.
Not turned into rows: LIVE rows with no defect clause; per-migration object ledgers (C2 section 4 sub-tables) and C1 section 5 object tables (their verdicts duplicate the migration STATUS rows); "ten rows that matter" and DISAGREES summary bullets that restate a table row (merged by similarity when both parsed); rule-register rows that are YES / n/a.
